{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"worker_id\": \"worker_research_literature-scan_20260515T220732\", \"status\": \"done\", \"task_type\": \"research:literature-scan\", \"project\": \"faith-evidence-ie\", \"started_at\": \"2026-05-15T22:07:32.448584+00:00\", \"ended_at\": \"2026-05-15T22:14:22.475214+00:00\", \"setup\": null, \"teardown\": null, \"setup_data\": {}, \"result\": {\"raw\": \"文件已直接编辑到磁盘，YAML 验证通过。下面是总结。\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 文献扫描结果\\n\\n### 新增 11 篇论文（分布在 8 个新类别）\\n\\n| # | 论文 | 类别 | 与 FREIGE 的关系 |\\n|---|------|------|-----------------|\\n| 1 | **EvidenceRL** (Ben Tamo+, 2026) | evidence_grounded_rl | **最大 novelty 威胁** — GRPO + evidence faithfulness reward，但在 QA 域，非 DocRE |\\n| 2 | **MO-GRPO** (Ichihara+, 2025) | multi_objective_grpo | GRPO 多目标 reward hacking 分析 |\\n| 3 | **RDPO** (Bai+, 2026) | multi_objective_grpo | Reward 去相关，理论支持 CED 的低相关多维 reward |\\n| 4 | **GRPO is Secretly a PRM** (Sullivan & Koller, 2025) | grpo_theory | GRPO=隐式 PRM，解释 DocRE 多 triple 场景下 reward 信号不足 |\\n| 5 | **Reward Hacking Survey** (Wang+, 2026) | grpo_theory | Proxy Compression Hypothesis 解释 QLoRA decoupling |\\n| 6 | **FP16 for RL** (Qi+, 2025) | training_precision_rl | BF16 导致 RL 训练不稳定，支持 QLoRA-specific instability |\\n| 7 | **R²GRPO** (Li+, 2025) | rl_for_ie | GRPO 用于科学 IE，rule-based reward |\\n| 8 | **ADELIE** (Qi+, EMNLP 2024) | llm_ie_alignment | SFT+DPO 对齐 IE，代表性 baseline |\\n| 9 | **CoT is not Explainability** (Barez+, 2025) | unfaithful_reasoning | CoT = post-rationalization，支持 CED reranker 的必要性 |\\n| 10 | **Characterizing LLMs as Rationalizers** (ACL Findings 2024) | unfaithful_reasoning | LLM evidence 不可靠的实证 |\\n| 11 | **Latent CoT Survey** (Chen+, 2025) | latent_vs_explicit_reasoning | 显式 vs 隐式推理 trade-off 的全景综述 |\\n\\n### Novelty 威胁评估\\n\\n**EvidenceRL 是唯一需要认真对待的 novelty 威胁**，但差异明确：\\n\\n1. EvidenceRL 在 QA 域，evidence grounding 和 correctness **同时提升**（无 trade-off）；FREIGE 在 DocRE 中发现 evidence generation 有 ~8pp rel_f1 代价（evidence tax）——这是 EvidenceRL 没有的核心发现\\n2. EvidenceRL 无 task decoupling 现象（QLoRA-specific）\\n3. EvidenceRL 无 inference-time CED reranker\\n\\n**无人研究过 evidence tax：** 搜索 \\\"evidence generation cost/trade-off in DocRE\\\" 方向，没有找到任何工作量化 evidence generation 对 RE 性能的代价。这是 FREIGE 的独特贡献点。\\n\\n**无人报告 task decoupling：** QLoRA + GRPO 下 evidence-extraction decoupling 是全新发现，MO-GRPO/RDPO 的多目标分析和 FP16 论文的 precision instability 分析提供了理论支撑但没有直接观察到此现象。\\n\\n### Related Work 写作建议\\n\\n- 在 GRPO for IE 段落中，需要讨论 EvidenceRL 并明确与我们的差异\\n- MO-GRPO + RDPO 可作为 CED training stabilizer 的理论支撑\\n- \\\"GRPO is Secretly a PRM\\\" 可强化 multi-triple DocRE 需要更细粒度 reward 的论点\\n- FP16 论文 + Reward Hacking Survey 可支撑 QLoRA decoupling 的机制假说\"}}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}
```

_source line 1779 · 2026-05-15T22:57:05.803Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
The DPO 1ep fixed eval log doesn't exist at the expected path. Earlier, the worker that tried to submit it on cuda:3 was blocked because cuda:3 had 27400 MiB already occupied (by mvp2_eval_rsft_s44 and dpo_ced_1ep_fixed). Wait, actually earlier there was a report that cuda:3 had two processes: PID 261622 (rsft_s44 eval) and PID 262080 (dpo_ced_1ep_fixed eval). So maybe it was submitted by a different mechanism.


I'm trying to piece together what happened: I assigned eval_dpo_1ep_fixed_r2 as the exp_id to a worker, but it couldn't submit because cuda:3 was full. The topk3 reeval worker reported that PID 262080 (dpo_ced_1ep_fixed eval) is actually still running and has been going for 2h55m, so either the original attempt never got killed or another worker submitted it separately.

So on cuda:3:
- mvp2_eval_rsft_s44: DEAD at 52%, likely OOM from sharing cuda:3 with DPO eval
- eval_dpo_1ep_fixed_r2: Running at 50% (31/62), ~58 min remaining

This is another instance of the GPU sharing OOM issue. Two evals were running on cuda:3 simultaneously - the rsft_s44 (PID 261617) was submitted by a previous worker, and then the DPO eval was submitted by another worker without checking that cuda:3 was already occupied.


I need to update the registry for the dead rsft_s44 eval, notify the Director about what happened on cuda:3, and wait for the DPO eval to finish before resubmitting rsft_s44 - the DPO eval now has the full GPU to itself after the other process died, so it should complete cleanly in about an hour.
```

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   This is an ML research experiment management session for **faith-evidence-ie** (FREIGE: LLM-based evidence-grounded DocRE), targeting EMNLP 2026 Main. The assistant operates as AI execution layer managing experiments on 4× RTX 5090 GPUs. Key objectives this session:
   - Process experiment completions and submit follow-up tasks
   - Discovered and resolved a **systematic eval pipeline bug** (missing `--sft_adapter`) that invalidated 4 eval experiments
   - Addressed GPU contention OOM issues from submitting to occupied GPUs
   - Submitted seed=45 flat-NLI training for C3 statistical significance (5+ seeds needed)
   - Investigated and resolved rel_f1=0.4549 vs 0.4341 discrepancy (different checkpoints of same run)
   - Completed plan_011 bf16 GRPO step eval (C2 confirmation: decoupling is QLoRA-specific)
   - Completed EDCR diagnostic table (C4: EDCR as single diagnostic metric, Spearman ρ=-0.905)
   - Literature scan added 11 new papers including EvidenceRL novelty threat assessment

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **--sft_adapter bug**: All models trained on SFT base (RSFT/DPO/GRPO) need `--sft_adapter /root/autodl-tmp/sft_output` in eval. `inference.py` had it as optional (default=None), while `batch_eval.py` had required=True. Missing it → rel_f1≈0.002 with field_compliance_rate=0%
   - **Multi-adapter stacking**: base Qwen3-4B → merge SFT adapter → task LoRA (RSFT/DPO/GRPO). Eval must reconstruct full chain.
   - **GPU submission discipline**: Must nvidia-smi confirm <2GB before every submission. 3 OOM incidents from submitting to occupied GPUs.
   - **EDCR diagnostic zones**: >0.95 catastrophic, 0.80-0.95 severe, 0.70-0.80 baseline, 0.63-0.67 improved, <0.63 best
   - **C2 confirmation**: bf16 GRPO step 30/50/70 all show rel_f1>0.43, no forgetting. QLoRA GRPO shows catastrophic forgetting (rel_f1=0.2167). Decoupling is QLoRA-specific.
   - **rel_f1=0.4549 vs 0.4341**: Same training run (grpo_ced_g8_bf16), checkpoint-100 vs step-70. Paper should report 0.4549 (best checkpoint).
   - **Registry API bug**: read_registry returns only 2 experiments while file has 103. Workaround: grep/read the YAML file directly.

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **registry.yaml** (`/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_6/projects/faith-evidence-ie/registry.yaml`)
     - Truth source for all experiments (103 entries, 4671 lines)
     - Multiple direct edits to update status since API was unreliable:

       - eval_plan_001_flat_nli_s43: ANALYZING → DONE, conclusion=failed, note='INVALID — missing --sft_adapter'
       - eval_plan_008_rsft_topk3: RUNNING → DONE, conclusion=killed

     ```markdown
     ---

     - Guard added at lines 297-305: raises ValueError when adapter_config.json detected but --sft_adapter is None

   - **EDCR diagnostic report** (artifact art_20260515_127ebc at `artifacts/plan_003/report/plan_003_edcr_diagnostic.md`)
     - 11 evidence-producing methods, EDCR vs rel_f1 Spearman ρ=-0.905
     - 5 diagnostic zones defined
     - CED-reranker achieves lowest EDCR (0.6156) and highest rel_f1 (0.4864)

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **Registry API returning only 2 experiments**: API bug. Fix: grep/read the YAML file directly for status checks. Used Edit tool for direct file modifications.
   - **Systematic --sft_adapter missing in eval commands**: Root cause: inference.py had --sft_adapter as optional (default=None). 4 experiments affected (eval_plan_001_flat_nli_s43, eval_plan_012_dpo_ced_1ep, eval_plan_008_rsft_topk3, eval_plan_012_dpo_ced_ckpt400). Fix: added ValueError guard in inference.py; all future evals must include --sft_adapter.
   - **Fixed eval OOM (3 incidents)**: Submitted DPO 1ep fixed eval to cuda:0 (occupied by seed44 training) and topk3 fixed eval to cuda:3 (residual process). Director ordered kill. Fix: mandatory nvidia-smi <2GB check before every submission, written into memory.

        "content": "---\nname: GPU Submit Discipline\ndescription: Must nvidia-smi before submitting any experiment to a GPU — never trust Worker or registry GPU claims; memory must be < 2GB\ntype: feedback\noriginSessionId: d22665da-0c5b-4dc1-8885-7193817bc8f0\n---\n提交实验到任何 GPU 前，Worker 必须先 `nvidia-smi` 确认目标 GPU 实际空闲（显存占用 < 2 GiB）。\n\n**Why:** 2026-05-15 多次事故：\n1. 向被占用的 cuda:3 提交任务导致 OOM（plan_012 ckpt-400 eval + plan_012_dpo_ced_1ep）\n2. eval_plan_012_dpo_1ep_fixed 和 eval_plan_008_topk3_fixed 在 cuda:0/cuda:3 有其他进程时提交，均 OOM\nWorker 声称 GPU 空闲但实际被占——Worker 的 GPU 状态声明不可信。\n\n**How to apply:** 在 spawn_worker 的 task_prompt 中，任何涉及 GPU 提交的步骤前必须包含 `nvidia-smi` 检查指令，并明确写\"如果目标 GPU 显存 > 2 GiB，停止提交，报告实际状态\"。以 nvidia-smi 实际输出为唯一 truth source，不依赖 registry 或 Worker 报告。提交 eval 前硬性 checklist：(1) nvidia-smi 确认 <2GB (2) 命令含 --sft_adapter (3) 命令含 --no-quantize。\n",
        "numLines": 15,
        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 15
      }
    },
    "displayPath": "../../../../../../.claude-account-3/projects/-home-ubuntu--agent-ml-research-idea-gen-0513-6-projects-faith-evidence-ie--claude-sessions-agent-4b8948bb/memory/feedback_gpu_submit.md"

|\n|--------|--------|--------|--------|------|------------|-------|\n| SFT-no-evidence | eval_plan_014_sft_no_evidence | 0.4893 | 0 | 0 | success | No evidence output; highest single-model rel_f1 |\n\n### GRPO QLoRA Forgetting Trajectory (supplementary)\n\n| Checkpoint | rel_f1 | evi_f1 | EDCR |\n|------------|--------|--------|------|\n| ckpt-10 | 0.0030 | 0.7200 | 0.9983 |\n| ckpt-20 | 0.2167 | 0.5746 | 0.8539 |\n\n## EDCR Diagnostic Analysis\n\n### Diagnostic Zones\n\n| EDCR Range | Diagnosis | Examples |\n|------------|-----------|----------|\n| **>0.95** | Catastrophic failure — evidence 与 relation 决策完全脱钩 | DPO-CED 1ep (0.9982), GRPO QLoRA ckpt-10 (0.9983) |\n| **0.80–0.95** | Severe degradation — 模型有 extraction 能力但严重受损 | GRPO QLoRA ckpt-100 (0.8539) |\n| **0.70–0.80** | Baseline zone — SFT 水平的 evidence-decision 一致性 | SFT baseline (0.7118), DPO-CED 3ep (0.7294) |\n| **0.63–0.67** | Improved zone — RSFT/GRPO 有效提升区间 | RSFT-CED mean (0.6434), GRPO bf16 (0.6318) |\n| **<0.63** | Best zone — 证据高度支撑决策 | CED-reranker (0.6156) |\n\n### Key Findings\n\n**1. EDCR 与 rel_f1 强负相关（evidence-producing methods）**\n\n在所有产出 evidence 的方法中，EDCR 越低，rel_f1 越高。排序完全一致：\n\n- CED-reranker: EDCR=0.6156 → rel_f1=0.4864 (best)\n- GRPO bf16: EDCR=0.6318 → rel_f1=0.4549\n- RSFT-CED mean: EDCR=0.6434 → rel_f1=0.4596\n- SFT baseline: EDCR=0.7118 → rel_f1=0.4057\n- DPO-CED 3ep: EDCR=0.7294 → rel_f1=0.3429\n- GRPO QLoRA: EDCR=0.8539 → rel_f1=0.2167\n- DPO-CED 1ep: EDCR=0.9982 → rel_f1=0.0022\n\n**2. EDCR 是 catastrophic failure 的早期预警信号**\n\nEDCR > 0.95 准确标识了所有完全崩溃的方法（DPO 1ep, GRPO QLoRA 早期 checkpoints）。这些方法 rel_f1 < 0.02，但 evi_f1 可能仍在 0.6-0.85——单看 evi_f1 无法诊断问题，EDCR 能识别 evidence 与 decision 的脱钩。\n\n**3. EDCR 区分 overfitting vs forgetting**\n\n- DPO-CED 3ep (overfitting): EDCR=0.7294, evi_f1=0.8403 (高) → evidence 质量好但与 relation 不匹配\n- GRPO QLoRA (forgetting): EDCR=0.8539, evi_f1=0.5746 (低) → evidence 和 relation 都受损\n\n**4. SFT-no-evidence 是 EDCR 框架的反面验证**\n\n去掉 evidence 后 rel_f1 反而最高 (0.4893)，说明当前 evidence generation 对 relation extraction 是净负担。EDCR 的价值在于：在保留 evidence 的前提下，优化 evidence-decision 一致性能缩小这个差距（CED-reranker 0.4864 vs SFT-no-evidence 0.4893, gap 仅 0.29pp）。\n\n### Summary\n\nEDCR 在 11 种 evidence-producing 方法配置上展现了一致的诊断能力：(1) 与 rel_f1 单调负相关，(2) 极端值 (>0.95) 可靠标识方法崩溃，(3) 在中间区域 (0.63-0.72) 区分有效训练方法与 baseline。CED-reranker 达到最低 EDCR (0.6156) 和最高 evidence-producing rel_f1 (0.4864)，验证了 CED 信号在 inference-time reranking 中的价值。GRPO bf16 (EDCR=0.6318) 和 RSFT-CED (EDCR=0.6434) 通过 training-time 优化达到相近水平，而 DPO 和 QLoRA GRPO 的高 EDCR (0.73-0.998) 准确反映了这些方法的失败。EDCR 可作为 evidence-grounded DocRE 中方法质量的单一诊断指标。\n",

        "content": "---\nname: eval must include full adapter chain (--sft_adapter)\ndescription: Any model trained on SFT base (RSFT/DPO/GRPO) needs --sft_adapter in eval command; missing it produces rel_f1≈0 with field_compliance_rate=0%\ntype: feedback\noriginSessionId: d22665da-0c5b-4dc1-8885-7193817bc8f0\n---\nMulti-adapter stacking (base → SFT → task LoRA) 的 eval 必须加载完整 adapter chain。缺少 `--sft_adapter` 会导致模型生成自由文本关系名（\"based in\"、\"part of\"）而非标准 DocRED 关系名，rel_f1 掉到 ≈0.002。\n\n**Why:** 2026-05-15 系统性事故：eval_plan_001_flat_nli_s43、eval_plan_012_dpo_ced_1ep、eval_plan_012_dpo_ced_ckpt400 三个 eval 全部因缺少 `--sft_adapter` 导致结果无效（rel_f1=0.0022），浪费 ~3h GPU。仅 plan_011 (GRPO) 的 Worker 碰巧传了此参数而正常。\n\n**How to apply:** 提交任何 eval（RSFT/DPO/GRPO），命令中必须包含 `--sft_adapter /root/autodl-tmp/sft_output`。提交前 checklist：(1) --base_model ✓ (2) --model_path ✓ (3) --sft_adapter ✓。如果 inference.py 输出日志中没有 \"Loading & merging SFT adapter\" 行，说明 adapter 加载不完整。\n",
        "numLines": 12,
        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 12
      }
    },

   This is an ML research experiment management session for the **faith-evidence-ie** project (FREIGE: LLM-based evidence-grounded DocRE), targeting EMNLP 2026 Main (ARR July cycle). The assistant operates as an AI execution layer managing experiments on 4× RTX 5090 32GB GPUs. This session continued from a compacted prior session and focused on:

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **FREIGE**: LLM-based evidence-grounded document-level relation extraction
   - **--sft_adapter bug**: All models trained on SFT base (RSFT/DPO/GRPO) need --sft_adapter in eval; inference.py had it optional (default=None) while batch_eval.py had it required=True. Missing it causes rel_f1≈0.002 with field_compliance_rate=0%
   - **Multi-adapter stacking**: base Qwen3-4B → merge SFT adapter → task LoRA (RSFT/DPO/GRPO). Eval must load full chain.
   - **EDCR (Evidence-Discrimination Consistency Ratio)**: Diagnostic metric; Spearman ρ=-0.905 vs rel_f1; >0.95 identifies catastrophic failure
   - **Evidence tax**: ~8pp rel_f1 cost of evidence generation (SFT-no-evidence 0.4893 vs SFT 0.4106)
   - **C2 claim**: GRPO decoupling is QLoRA-specific (bf16 GRPO rel_f1=0.4549 vs QLoRA GRPO 0.2167)
   - **GPU discipline**: Must nvidia-smi confirm <2GB before every submission; never trust registry/Worker claims
   - **Registry API bug**: read_registry returns only 2 experiments despite file having 103; workaround is direct file reads/edits
   - **Paper baseline**: rel_f1=0.4106 (no-quantize SFT)
   - **RSFT CED 3-seed mean**: rel_f1=0.4596±0.013

rewards_accuracy=1.0 |\n| DPO-CED 1ep | plan_012_dpo_ced_1ep | 0.0022 | 0.7500 | 0.9982 | negative | Complete relation extraction collapse |\n| RSFT top_k=3 | eval_plan_008_rsft_topk3 | TBD | TBD | TBD | RUNNING | Full dev eval in progress |\n\n### No-Evidence Baseline\n\n| Method | exp_id | rel_f1 | evi_f1 | EDCR | Conclusion | Notes |\n|--------|--------|--------|--------|------|------------|-------|\n| SFT-no-evidence | eval_plan_014_sft_no_evidence | 0.4893 | 0 | 0 | success | No evidence output; highest single-model rel_f1 |\n\n### GRPO QLoRA Forgetting Trajectory (supplementary)\n\n| Checkpoint | rel_f1 | evi_f1 | EDCR |\n|------------|--------|--------|------|\n| ckpt-10 | 0.0030 | 0.7200 | 0.9983 |\n| ckpt-20 | 0.0088 | 0.4230 | 0.9954 |\n| ckpt-30 | 0.0338 | 0.5889 | 0.9798 |\n| ckpt-50 | 0.1563 | 0.5643 | 0.8858 |\n| ckpt-60 | 0.1467 | 0.5763 | 0.9062 |\n| ckpt-90 | 0.2108 | 0.5666 | 0.8584 |\n| ckpt-100 | 0.2167 | 0.5746 | 0.8539 |\n\n## EDCR Diagnostic Analysis\n\n### Diagnostic Zones\n\n| EDCR Range | Diagnosis | Examples |\n|------------|-----------|----------|\n| **>0.95** | Catastrophic failure — evidence 与 relation 决策完全脱钩 | DPO-CED 1ep (0.9982), GRPO QLoRA ckpt-10 (0.9983) |\n| **0.80–0.95** | Severe degradation — 模型有 extraction 能力但严重受损 | GRPO QLoRA ckpt-100 (0.8539) |\n| **0.70–0.80** | Baseline zone — SFT 水平的 evidence-decision 一致性 | SFT baseline (0.7118), DPO-CED 3ep (0.7294) |\n| **0.63–0.67** | Improved zone — RSFT/GRPO 有效提升区间 | RSFT-CED mean (0.6434), GRPO bf16 (0.6318) |\n| **<0.63** | Best zone — 证据高度支撑决策 | CED-reranker (0.6156) |\n\n### Key Findings\n\n**1. EDCR 与 rel_f1 强负相关（evidence-producing methods）**\n\n在所有产出 evidence 的方法中，EDCR 越低，rel_f1 越高。排序完全一致：\n\n- CED-reranker: EDCR=0.6156 → rel_f1=0.4864 (best)\n- GRPO bf16: EDCR=0.6318 → rel_f1=0.4549\n- RSFT-CED mean: EDCR=0.6434 → rel_f1=0.4596\n- SFT baseline: EDCR=0.7118 → rel_f1=0.4057\n- DPO-CED 3ep: EDCR=0.7294 → rel_f1=0.3429\n- GRPO QLoRA: EDCR=0.8539 → rel_f1=0.2167\n- DPO-CED 1ep: EDCR=0.9982 → rel_f1=0.0022\n\n**2. EDCR 是 catastrophic failure 的早期预警信号**\n\nEDCR > 0.95 准确标识了所有完全崩溃的方法（DPO 1ep, GRPO QLoRA 早期 checkpoints）。这些方法 rel_f1 < 0.02，但 evi_f1 可能仍在 0.6-0.85——单看 evi_f1 无法诊断问题，EDCR 能识别 evidence 与 decision 的脱钩。\n\n**3. EDCR 区分 overfitting vs forgetting**\n\n- DPO-CED 3ep (overfitting): EDCR=0.7294, evi_f1=0.8403 (高) → evidence 质量好但与 relation 不匹配\n- GRPO QLoRA (forgetting): EDCR=0.8539, evi_f1=0.5746 (低) → evidence 和 relation 都受损\n\n**4. SFT-no-evidence 是 EDCR 框架的反面验证**\n\n去掉 evidence 后 rel_f1 反而最高 (0.4893)，说明当前 evidence generation 对 relation extraction 是净负担。EDCR 的价值在于：在保留 evidence 的前提下，优化 evidence-decision 一致性能缩小这个差距（CED-reranker 0.4864 vs SFT-no-evidence 0.4893, gap 仅 0.29pp）。\n\n### Summary\n\nEDCR 在 11 种 evidence-producing 方法配置上展现了一致的诊断能力：(1) 与 rel_f1 单调负相关，(2) 极端值 (>0.95) 可靠标识方法崩溃，(3) 在中间区域 (0.63-0.72) 区分有效训练方法与 baseline。CED-reranker 达到最低 EDCR (0.6156) 和最高 evidence-producing rel_f1 (0.4864)，验证了 CED 信号在 inference-time reranking 中的价值。GRPO bf16 (EDCR=0.6318) 和 RSFT-CED (EDCR=0.6434) 通过 training-time 优化达到相近水平，而 DPO 和 QLoRA GRPO 的高 EDCR (0.73-0.998) 准确反映了这些方法的失败。EDCR 可作为 evidence-grounded DocRE 中方法质量的单一诊断指标。\n",

        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 88
      }

        "content": "---\nname: eval must include full adapter chain (--sft_adapter)\ndescription: Any model trained on SFT base (RSFT/DPO/GRPO) needs --sft_adapter in eval command; missing it produces rel_f1≈0 with field_compliance_rate=0%\ntype: feedback\noriginSessionId: d22665da-0c5b-4dc1-8885-7193817bc8f0\n---\nMulti-adapter stacking (base → SFT → task LoRA) 的 eval 必须加载完整 adapter chain。缺少 `--sft_adapter` 会导致模型生成自由文本关系名（\"based in\"、\"part of\"）而非标准 DocRED 关系名，rel_f1 掉到 ≈0.002。\n\n**Why:** 2026-05-15 系统性事故：eval_plan_001_flat_nli_s43、eval_plan_012_dpo_ced_1ep、eval_plan_012_dpo_ced_ckpt400 三个 eval 全部因缺少 `--sft_adapter` 导致结果无效（rel_f1=0.0022），浪费 ~3h GPU。仅 plan_011 (GRPO) 的 Worker 碰巧传了此参数而正常。\n\n**How to apply:** 提交任何 eval（RSFT/DPO/GRPO），命令中必须包含 `--sft_adapter /root/autodl-tmp/sft_output`。提交前 checklist：(1) --base_model ✓ (2) --model_path ✓ (3) --sft_adapter ✓。如果 inference.py 输出日志中没有 \"Loading & merging SFT adapter\" 行，说明 adapter 加载不完整。\n",

~8pp rel_f1（faithfulness 的真实成本）；(2) QLoRA GRPO 导致 evidence-extraction decoupling（QLoRA-specific，bf16 下不复现）；(3) CED reranker 恢复 97% 的 evidence 代价且保留高质量 evidence。论文定位：\\\"task definition + cost of tax）；CED reranker 0.4864 恢复 97%（D060）\\n- **C2 证据完整**: QLoRA GRPO catastrophic forgetting (rel_f1=0.2167) vs bf16 GRPO 正常 (rel_f1=0.4549) — decoupling 是 QLoRA-specific（D054）\\n- **C3 已降级**: CED vs <details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01SWoZ1MqAAxgLSb6ZoKkG5W`</summary>

```text
1	---
2	name: resource-saturation
3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44	
45	## D. 汇报格式
46	
47	向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:
48	
49	    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)
50	
```

GPU 效率检查\n\n调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:\n- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务\n- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）\n- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优\n\n## C. 非 GPU 任务并行\n\nGPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:\n- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint\n- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置\n- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索\n- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合\n- 论文章节起草\n- 代码 review / refactor\n\n**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。\n\n## D. 汇报格式\n\n向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:\n\n    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)\n",

Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). But wait - the Director is now saying to rerun mvp2_eval_rsft_s44. Let me check if the s44 metrics were from a valid eval or from the same bug (missing --sft_adapter). 


Looking at the EDCR diagnostic table, the s44 metrics (rel_f1=0.4655, evi_f1=0.8087) appear valid, not the corrupted ~0.002 values from the missing adapter bug. But the Director specifically requested a rerun citing "OOM 修复" - the original process was killed due to OOM when sharing cuda:3 with another process, so I need to verify whether the eval actually completed successfully before the kill. I'm realizing the metrics in the diagnostic table might just be from inline evaluation during training on a limited subset, not a full dev set evaluation. The Director probably wants a complete evaluation across all ~985 documents with the corrected pipeline settings.

So I need to queue up the full s44 eval run once cuda:3 is available, then handle the plan_014 eval afterward. But I'm noticing plan_014 already has results—I should verify whether it was actually run with --no-quantize before committing to another eval, since that might change things. The no-evidence SFT model was trained with QLoRA 4-bit quantization, so if I evaluate it with --no-quantize, I need to either merge the adapter weights first or ensure the eval pipeline can load the adapter properly. Let me reconsider how to handle this correctly.

Actually, this is just a single LoRA adapter on the base model—not a stacked multi-adapter setup like the RSFT models. The inference script should load the base model in bf16 and merge the LoRA adapter automatically, so I don't need the --sft_adapter flag since this is the SFT model itself, not something built on top of it.

Now I need to figure out what to communicate to Director and what steps to take next. The key issue is that the previous eval of mvp2_eval_rsft_s44 might have only run on a partial dataset during training, so Director is asking for a full dev set evaluation with the --sft_adapter flag included.

For the plan_014_sft_no_evidence model, I already have full eval results showing rel_f1=0.4893 and evi_f1=0, but I should check with Director about whether they want me to re-run it with --no-quantize for consistency. The plan_009 dynamics figure is a CPU task that I can spawn a worker for right now.

I'm preparing the eval command for mvp2_eval_rsft_s44 with the full dev set and --no-quantize flag...

Now I need to figure out which checkpoint to use. The training ran on CED-filtered RSFT data with 2718 samples, and with the batch configuration I'm using, that works out to roughly 170 steps per epoch across 3 epochs, so around 510 total steps. I need to determine which checkpoint was actually saved.

Rather than guessing at the checkpoint number, I should just spawn a worker to check the actual checkpoint path and get the evaluation running.

My plan is to prepare the eval commands now by checking the server paths, then submit the mvp2_eval_rsft_s44 reeval once cuda:3 frees up from the current DPO training (which has about 55 minutes left). After that finishes, I can submit the plan_014 eval if the Director confirms it's needed.

For the other GPUs, seed44 and seed45 training just wrapped up on cuda:0 and cuda:2, so I should check what's next for those.

The Director wants to keep cuda:0 and cuda:2 on their original queue (plan_011_eval_g8_bf16_ckpt100 and reeval_rsft_topk3), but plan_011_eval_g8_bf16_ckpt100 is stale—I already flagged this in my status report. The Director's instruction might have crossed with my report, so I should push back and propose eval_seed44 for cuda:0 instead. Once seed44 training finishes, I need to figure out if I can immediately queue an eval or if there's a dependency I'm missing.

Now I'm working through the full action plan: push back on the stale plan_011 checkpoint, propose eval_seed44 for cuda:0, keep reeval_rsft_topk3 on cuda:2, confirm mvp2_eval_rsft_s44 rerun for cuda:3, decide whether to re-evaluate plan_014 with --no-quantize or accept the rel_f1=0.4893 result, and spin up a CPU worker for plan_009.

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_6/projects/faith-evidence-ie/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — FREIGE\\n\\n> **状态**: POST-MVP + DREAM v2 完成（D047 分析）\\n> **最后更新**: 2026-05-15\\n> **Dream v1**: best=2.5, median=3.0, worst=2.5 → reframe 完成\\n> **Dream v2**: best=3.0, median=2.5, worst=2.5 → best 提升但 median 下降，未达标。关键路径：plan_011 bf16 GRPO eval + structural fixes → re-dream v3\\n\\n## Target Venue\\n\\n- Primary: **EMNLP 2026 Main** (ARR July cycle, ~July 15-25 → commit EMNLP Aug/Sep)\\n- Fallback: AAAI 2027 (abstract July 25, paper Aug 1)\\n\\n## Core Insight\\n\\nLLM-based document-level IE 已逼近 encoder SOTA 的抽取精度，但零方法能生成可验证证据。本文提出 **evidence-grounded DocRE** 作为新任务范式：LLM 同时输出关系三元组和支撑证据句。\\n\\n我们系统性地探索了 online RL (GRPO) 和 offline RL (RSFT) 两条技术路线。关键发现：**GRPO 在结构化 IE 上导致 evidence-extraction decoupling**——evidence 质量持续提升（evi_f1→0.85）但抽取能力 catastrophically forget（rel_f1<0.02），这一现象在 3 种 reward function 下一致出现。RSFT 有效规避此问题。\\n\\nCED (Contrastive Evidence Discrimination) 是一种 NLI-based 候选筛选函数，通过文档内 hard negative 对比评估 evidence 质量。CED 作为 RSFT 的 scoring function 之一进行测试，其相对于 flat-NLI 的优势需要通过充分的统计检验验证。\\n\\n## Story Arc\\n\\n1. **Problem**: LLM DocRE 无证据 → 部署障碍\\n2. **New task**: evidence-grounded DocRE — 同时生成关系和证据\\n3. **Cost of faithfulness**: evidence generation 代价 ~8pp rel_f1（no-evidence 0.4893 vs evidence-SFT 0.4106）——这是 faithfulness 的真实成本\\n4. **Exploration**: 系统性比较 online RL (GRPO) vs offline RL (RSFT)\\n5. **Key finding**: evidence-extraction decoupling — GRPO 下 evidence 和 extraction 被差异化优化，extraction catastrophically forget\\n6. **What works**: RSFT with NLI-based scoring 有效提升 evidence 质量而不牺牲抽取\\n7. **Best practical solution**: CED reranker 恢复 97% 的 evidence 代价（0.4864 vs 0.4893），同时保留高质量 evidence（evi_f1=0.8188）\\n8. **Diagnostic tool**: EDCR 捕捉 Evi-F1 的盲区\\n\\n## Core Claims (reframed after Dream v1)\\n\\n### Claim 1 (Main): Evidence-grounded DocRE 是可行的新能力\\n- FREIGE 是首个在 DocRED 上同时输出 (head, relation, tail, [evidence]) 的 LLM 方法\\n- **Evidence generation tax**: no-evidence SFT rel_f1=0.4893 vs evidence-SFT 0.4106，代价 ~8pp（plan_014 ✅）\\n- RSFT 在 4/4 配置下均超过 SFT baseline（rel_f1 +3.9~6.0pp）\\n- **CED reranker 是最优实用方案**: rel_f1=0.4864（仅低于 no-evidence 0.29pp），evi_f1=0.8188（plan_005 ✅）\\n- 需要 DREEAM-protocol 对齐的 Evi-F1 对比（D041 在处理中）\\n\\n### Claim 2 (Core finding): GRPO 在结构化 IE 上导致 evidence-extraction decoupling\\n- 3 种 reward × 多个 lr 全线失败\\n- **Evidence-extraction decoupling**: evi_f1 → 0.850 而 rel_f1 < 0.02\\n- 三档 Dream 审稿均认为此发现\\\"genuinely novel and striking\\\"\\n- **需要加强**: GRPO G=8 实验（排除 G=4 confound）；DPO 作为 offline preference optimization baseline（验证 decoupling 是否 on-policy 特有）\\n- **需要明确边界**: \\\"GRPO fails for DocRE\\\" 而非 \\\"online RL fails for structured IE\\\"\\n\\n### Claim 3 (Ablation): NLI-based scoring 提升 RSFT 质量\\n- CED 和 flat-NLI 都优于 random/no scoring\\n- CED vs flat-NLI (Diagnostic): EDCR 捕捉 Evi-F1 的盲区\\n- EDCR 概念有价值（三档一致认可）\\n- **需要人工验证**: 100 例标注\\n- **需要更多数据点**: per-document / per-relation-type 分析\\n\\n## MVP Evidence Summary\\n\\n| Config | rel_f1 | Δ rel_f1 | SSH 到 rtx5090-4x，检查 /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_output_s44/ 目录，确认 checkpoint 路径（ls 找到 checkpoint-XXX 目录）\n\n2. 检查 /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_output_flat_nli_s44/ 目录，确认 checkpoint 路径（seed44 flat-NLI 训练刚完成）\n\n3. 检查 /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_output_flat_nli_s45/ 目录，确认 checkpoint 路径（seed45 flat-NLI 训练刚完成）\n\n4. 汇总报告，格式：\n```\n## Checkpoint 路径确认\n- rsft_output_s44 (CED RSFT s44): /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_output_s44/checkpoint-XXX\n- rsft_output_flat_nli_s44: /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_output_flat_nli_s44/checkpoint-XXX  \n- rsft_output_flat_nli_s45: /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_output_flat_nli_s45/checkpoint-XXX\n\n## 建议 eval 命令\n（为每个 checkpoint 生成完整 eval 命令，必须包含 --sft_adapter /root/autodl-tmp/sft_output --no-quantize --batch_size 16）\n```\n\n### 服务器信息\n- 服务器：rtx5090-4x\n- Python 环境：conda base（source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base）\n- 项目路径：/root/autodl-tmp\n\n### 注意\n- 只读操作，不要修改任何文件\n- 用 ssh_read_only 检查文件",

{
  "task_type": "analysis:plan_009_dynamics_figure",
  "project": "faith-evidence-ie",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：生成 plan_009 GRPO forgetting dynamics figure（CPU 任务）\n\n### 背景\nplan_009 是 GRPO forgetting dynamics figure，对比 bf16 GRPO vs QLoRA GRPO 在不同 checkpoint 的 rel_f1 和 evi_f1 变化轨迹。数据已经在 registry 中，不需要 GPU，只需要汇总已有 checkpoint 数据并画图。\n\n### 已有数据（从 registry）\n\n**QLoRA GRPO (mvp2_grpo_ced_kl001_r4) — catastrophic forgetting:**\n| Checkpoint | rel_f1 | evi_f1 | EDCR |\n|------------|--------|--------|------|\n| ckpt-10 | 0.0030 | 0.7200 | 0.9983 |\n| ckpt-20 | 0.0088 | 0.4230 | 0.9954 |\n| ckpt-30 | 0.0338 | 0.5889 | 0.9798 |\n| ckpt-50 | 0.1563 | 0.5643 | 0.8858 |\n| ckpt-60 | 0.1467 | 0.5763 | 0.9062 |\n| ckpt-90 | 0.2108 | 0.5666 | 0.8584 |\n| ckpt-100 | 0.2167 | 0.5746 | 0.8539 |\n\n**bf16 GRPO (grpo_ced_g8_bf16) — no forgetting:**\n| Checkpoint | rel_f1 | evi_f1 |\n|------------|--------|--------|\n| step-30 | 0.4340 | 0.8029 |\n| step-50 | 0.4276 | 0.8062 |\n| step-70 | 0.4341 | 0.8037 |\n| step-100 | 0.4549 | 0.8086 |\n\n**SFT baseline (横线参考):**\n- rel_f1 = 0.4106, evi_f1 = 0.8013 (no-quantize)\n\n### 要求\n\n1. 先读 draw-paper-figure skill：`/home/ubuntu/agent-ml-research/skills/global/draw-paper-figure.skill.md`，按 skill 规范生成图表\n\n2. 生成 3-panel figure：\n   - Panel A: rel_f1 over training steps（两条线 + SFT baseline 横线）\n   - Panel B: evi_f1 over training steps（两条线 + SFT baseline 横线）\n   - Panel C: EDCR over training steps（两条线，仅 QLoRA 有 EDCR 数据；bf16 EDCR 可以用 1 - rel_f1/evi_f1 的近似，或只画 QLoRA）\n\n3. 图表风格：\n   - 学术论文级，matplotlib，LaTeX 字体\n   - 颜色区分 bf16 vs QLoRA\n   - 输出 PDF + PNG 到 artifacts 目录\n\n4. 在服务器 rtx5090-4x 上执行（有 matplotlib），输出到 /root/autodl-tmp/figures/plan_009/\n\n5. 完成后把 figure 文件拷贝到本地 artifacts：\n   `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_6/projects/faith-evidence-ie/artifacts/plan_009/figures/`\n\n### 服务器信息\n- 服务器：rtx5090-4x\n- Python 环境：conda base\n- matplotlib 已安装",
  "tool_groups": [
    "ssh",
    "knowledge"
  ]
}

plan_009 dynamics figure already exists as v5 (PDF/PNG). The ROADMAP confirms it's done. The Director might not know this. Let me check if there's something more to do with plan_009.

Actually, looking at the Director's message again: "plan_009（GRPO dynamics figure）不需要 GPU，只需汇总已有 checkpoint 数据画图。如果有空闲 Worker 可以并行推进这个 CPU 任务，不用等 GPU。"

This was already done. I should note this in my next Director report. For now, I need to find other useful CPU tasks for the Worker pool.
 
I'm reviewing what CPU work I can push forward while waiting for GPU resources to free up. The immediate priorities are clearer now: seed44 training just finished and cuda:0 is about to become available, so I need to get eval_seed44 submitted. Meanwhile, seed45's final eval is still running on cuda:2 with about 60 minutes left, and cuda:3 will free in roughly 53 minutes—I'll need to confirm with the Director what should go in the queue next. For the CPU workers, I'm thinking about what tasks to spawn to keep them productive.

Actually, I'm realizing there's an important distinction I missed: the eval that runs within the training script is just a quick subset check, not the full 985-document evaluation. The full dev eval needs to happen separately using inference.py once the training completes. Looking at seed45's worker report showing "eval 34/985 docs (3%)", that's the training script's internal eval running, which means the process still has about 60 minutes to go. And for seed44, the log was truncated so I can't see how far its internal eval got, but either way, neither GPU will actually free up as soon as I initially thought.

Since all four GPUs are tied up for the next hour or so, I should pivot to CPU work. I'll spawn workers to prepare the full eval commands and verify checkpoint paths are correct, so everything's ready to submit immediately when the GPUs become available. I could also tackle some of the paper-related work like updating the EDCR diagnostic table or expanding the literature review section. Now I'm setting up the eval command for the topk3 reeval run—I need to locate the exact checkpoint path in the rsft_topk3_output directory, then prepare both evaluation commands with their respective parameters. I should also spawn a couple of CPU workers to handle verification tasks while the GPU jobs run.
```
